## v1.0.20 — 2026-07-25

### Features
- add checkout workflow helper utility (#3660) (4080af37)
- add nightly-bug-sweep JobDefinition (live test for jobs feature) (2dba5664)
- migrate all remaining active .md duties -> folder+profile (67043134)
- migrate dev-ci-health -> locked-toolbox folder-duty + pin next.9 (14759f64)
- migrate security-audit .md -> stateful folder+profile (5edf626f)
- migrate health-check .md -> folder+profile (fd821ec9)
- convert all remaining executables to duties (folder move) (f42bef7d)
- move feature from executables to duties + pin engine next.2 (5df8e58b)
- convert executables to duties (assign existing staff) (8b7d49ec)
- ui-review runs as designer (c3f45264)
- nightly-tests runs as qa (14b1b486)
- qa-engineer runs as qa (7cdd6aba)
- reproduce runs as qa (0a716e47)
- classify runs as engineer (df71cadd)
- fix-ci runs as engineer (30696eaf)
- chore runs as engineer (b961b4c7)
- fix runs as engineer (28320284)
- feature runs as engineer (d4e97968)
- bug runs as engineer (63b0e29f)
- review runs as cto (a3ceacfb)
- research runs as cto (b5d74728)
- spec runs as cto (843cd49c)
- plan runs as cto (4d037ec1)
- add designer persona for executable→duty conversion (1b083dd0)
- add qa persona for executable→duty conversion (052a7b3d)
- add engineer persona for executable→duty conversion (0fe04c53)
- add mcp-livetest (35e4767f)
- add company-verify (10488538)
- add per-task memory artifacts protocol + extractor job (5ffd7cfe)
- add CEO persona + job-gap-scan job (1ea5396d)
- add agent memory protocol + fix writer commit step (279437e6)
- add memory-writer job (b9733574)
- introduce sticky-note inbox layout (292c6a98)
- add title input for chat session naming (f3eed381)

### Fixes
- update homepage verification welcome text (#3899) (6292e0e2)
- align Kody with default branch (9302dbe8)
- run latest engine — add consumer executables + pin @latest (e577ef34)
- Accept numeric confidence values from LLM recs (4215d228)
- prevent duplicate recommendations after operator dismissal (cc329bfa)
- mention operator in recs; reset state to re-notify (1de517eb)
- rm pip cache key file after setup-python (68843b45)
- write pip cache key outside workspace (f9442d08)
- bump to 0.3.77 + checkout dispatch ref for chat sessions (adce6318)
- demote no-this-alias to warn (false positive on JSX) (0dfd2cb5)
- bring impls in line with their test specs (b35de5b1)

### Refactoring
- migrate kody assets to capabilities (dd6acde0)
- collapse memory system to direct task-extractor path (8dd48cc8)

### Docs
- add docs/stack-v10.md with step 1 seed (#3341) (4a847afe)
- add stack-v9.md seed document (#3326) (c07d6003)

## v1.0.11 — 2026-05-05

### Features
- replace single-stage cases with feature.full-flow (b32ecd27)
- add plan, classify, review live cases + PR-fixture support (bba2602b)
- add live tier with fixture lifecycle + failure-issue reporting (288cedc9)
- add nightly Kody engine smoke suite as a consumer-repo executable (e2d979ef)
- seed auto-resolve mission for live test (f1c55810)
- seed auto-fix-ci mission for live test (99738842)

### Fixes
- find PR via kody-posted URL comment, not branch search (18626a87)
- bulletproof run fixture, classify assertion (c24deb79)
- assert on RUN_COMPLETED, not DONE (90d78d3d)
- calibrate cases.json against engine v0.3.40 actuals (0ff0f839)
- explicit pip install litellm before engine (9c39ab0a)
- unpack secrets and set GH_TOKEN before invoking engine (17f92f83)

### Refactoring
- drop kody-nightly-tests.yml — engine fans out from kody.yml (6fc1f3a0)
